# Effect cleanup queues

The fold queue, defect queue and re-triage list for step 9 of `effect-cleanup-refactor.md`, merged on 2026-11-23 from the triage
runs and parallel triage chunks. They live here so the plan stays short: a run reads the plan, then only its own run's rows here
(grep for its run number, batch or class). Update the rows a run takes when it lands (fold runs: the batches folded; defect runs:
the defects fixed, with the commit).

A row's "plan in 23c Findings", "see 28's Findings" and the like name a run's Findings section in the plan. Since the plan's cut of
2026-11-25 those sections are in git history: `git show ef0278a44e:effect-cleanup-refactor.md` (runs from 2026-11-23 on; grep for
the heading, e.g. `### Step 9, fold run 23c`) and `git show 6728593a9a:effect-cleanup-refactor.md` (older runs). The plan keeps one
line per run under its Findings.

### Fold queue

Merged on 2026-11-23 from triage runs 1 to 7 and parallel triage chunks 01 to 16, whose Findings sections are in git history
(`git show 51f0afd546:effect-cleanup-refactor.md`; grep it for a class or card). One row per survivor or shared component. Every class
whose latest ledger row is `step-9` is in exactly one row (1,142 from the triage, the 7 older rows, and the 102 rows this consolidation
re-triaged, marked *: 1,251; and the 71 rows defect run D10 re-verdicted once the owner settled the hidden-zone logging question,
marked †: 1,322). The member's ledger note says how it folds; read it, its record and its handler before folding.

Needs: **P** parameters only (existing records), **C** a new component or option on the survivor, **S** a new or widened subject,
**K** a new step kind or step, **R** a new shared record, **St** a store merge, **Sp** a split onto existing steps, **D** delete.
Rows (cards), cost-shape: the batch's ledger rows, the cards that use them, and how many members' notes say `cost-shape` (cs). The `p-…`,
`splits`, `…-singles` and `weigh` rows gather records that fold onto different existing heads, each named in its note. Run: the fold
run below.

**Where the sections disagree** (the newer reading is taken unless marked check):

- `zone-play`: `ControllerCantCastSpellsFromHandEffect` and `ControllerCantPlayLandsFromHandEffect` fold onto
  `PlayersCantCastSpellsFromZonesEffect` (P05) or onto `ControllerCantPlayLandsEffect` with a `PlayerRestriction` (P07, whose rows they
  are). Check.
- `attack-together`: Magnetic Web's record is the survivor with Ekundu Cyclops and War's Toll (P06) or folds with Viashino Bey onto
  `MustAttackIfAnotherCreatureAttacksEffect` (P08). Check.
- `land-mana`: the floated land-mana records (Hall of Gemstone, Harvest Mage, Chaos Moon, Pale Moon, Deep Water) fold onto
  `ReplaceLandManaWithColorEffect` floated (P04, P05, P08) or onto `AllLandsProduceChosenColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect`, Hall of Gemstone's
  own record, with a land filter (P09, P13). Check.
- `land-type-static`: `BasicLandsOfChosenTypesBecomeTypeEffect` goes onto `GrantSubtypeEffect` with the chosen subtypes (P11) or onto
  `NonbasicLandsBecomeTypeEffect` with a land predicate (P09); `ControlledLandsBecomeTypeEffect` onto
  `GrantSubtypeEffect(PLAINS, landsYouControl(), true)` (P05) or the same setter (P09). Check.
- `vote`: P10 proposes a two-option `VoteEffect` with a payload per outcome, P12 a step recording `StepKind.VOTE` (votes per option,
  read by conditions and amounts), P14 per-option counts for council's dilemma, and P11's blocked secret votes need a tally per
  candidate. One step kind with per-option tallies covers all four; P10's payload form is its common case. Check the shape first.
- `opp-card`: `IntuitionEffect` is the survivor for Burning-Rune Demon (P11) or splits onto the SEARCH step and a `ChooseCardEffect` Done in fold run 10b.
  chosen by an opponent (P13, newer): P13 taken, Burning-Rune Demon follows it.
- `goad`: T1 gave `GoadSourceCreatureWhileControllerControlsItEffect` a static subject on `GoadEquippedCreatureEffect`; P08 (newer: its
  goad is made by a resolution) folds it onto `GoadTargetCreatureUntilNextTurnEffect` with a `Duration`. P08 taken.
- `conjure-dup`: `ConjureDuplicateOfTargetCreatureCardIntoGraveyardEffect` joins Sarevok's seek (T7) or the duplicate-destination
  batch (P16, newer). P16 taken.
- `suspect`: P08 said nothing reads `SuspectedEffect`'s float; P14 that `LayerSystemService` does. The code agrees with P14
  (`LayerSystemService`, `UnsuspectEffectHandler`, `GameQueryService`); it folds with the suspect batch, not as dead.
- `two-dice`, `life-to-loss`, `attack-together`: `ArcaneEndeavorEffect` (P10 unique), `OpponentLifeGainBecomesLifeLossEffect` (P02
  unique) and War's Toll's `OpponentCreaturesAttackTogetherEffect` (P02 unique) gained users in P15, P14 and P06: re-triaged.
- `search-step`: T2 counts run 29's `SearchLibraryForEquipmentToBattlefieldAndAttachEffect` in both the SEARCH step and the attach-host
  batch; it is placed with the SEARCH step, which it needs first. T2 also asks the orchestrator to confirm that the search completion
  makes the search an earlier step before the step is built.
- `exiled-store`: T4 leaves to the fold run whether the one store for counters on exiled cards is in scope (`blocked-other` if not);
  T5, P01, P08, P09 and P16 put more stores and records on it (Rex's brain, Mairsil's cage, dream, hatching, silver, egg).
- `attack-ban`: Chronomantic Escape's `CreaturesCantAttackControllerUntilNextTurnEffect` is floated by `GrantStaticEffectToPlayerEffect`
  (P06), which P06 says P02's attack-ban batch should take as its survivor: placed in `attack-ban`.

**Fold runs.** About 15 to 30 rows each, in this order. Group A (runs 1 to 10): dead records, parameters only and splits onto existing
steps. Group B (11 to 20): the parts that unblock other rows (VOTE, CONJURE, SEARCH, TAP, GOAD, the pile step, the attach verb, the
counter store with the static card subjects, the copier subject, the free-cast choice) and the batches that fix a listed defect on the
way (Ring, undying, reselect, the attached-copy tokens, the opponent-chosen mode, tempting offer, can't-be-countered, the seek event).
Group C (21 to 46): the other batches by area. Group D (47 to 49): `cost-shape` batches that overlap step 10's cost-combinator merge;
take them with step 10 or after its sign-off.

1 (A, 28) Dead records and by-parameter singles (library, cost); 2 (A, 32) By-parameter singles: damage, life, draw, boost; 3 (A, 29) By-parameter singles: permanents; 4 (A, 28) By-parameter rows: control, search, shuffle, copy, P/T, seek; 5 (A, 27) By-parameter rows: player floats, random, exile casts; 6 (A, 30) By-parameter rows: small existing heads; 7 (A, 27) By-parameter rows: small existing heads (2); 8 (A, 39) Splits onto existing steps; 9 (A, 29) Sacrifice, look and draw splits; 10 (A, 24) Opponent choices and small splits; 11 (B, 19) VOTE step; 12 (B, 22) CONJURE step and the drafted cards; 12a (B, 29) Conjures into hands and libraries; 12b (B, 29) Perpetual changes to cards in hands and libraries; 13 (B, 21) SEARCH step; 14 (B, 23) New step kinds: TAP, GOAD, piles, auction; 15 (B, 33) Attach verb; 16 (B, 23) Counters on exiled cards and the static card subjects; 17 (B, 30) Copier subject and copy exceptions; 18 (B, 25) Free-cast choice and graveyard casts; 19 (B, 19) Fixes a listed defect on the way; 20 (B, 27) Seek step (records SEEK and the sought event); 21 (C, 29) Shuffles and search variants; 22 (C, 26) Counter placement and tokens; 23 (C, 24) Counter moves and removal; 24 (C, 19) Counters on subjects and sets; 25 (C, 32) Damage modifiers; 26 (C, 23) Damage dealers; 27 (C, 28) Redirects and prevention; 28 (C, 26) Delayed abilities, next-time and bindings; 29 (C, 20) Spell copies and card copies; 30 (C, 26) Cast permissions; 31 (C, 25) Colour, P/T and names; 32 (C, 27) Types and animation; 33 (C, 21) Mana; 34 (C, 26) Player restrictions; 35 (C, 24) Player statics; 36 (C, 26) Combat restrictions; 37 (C, 25) Combat permissions and locks; 38 (C, 23) Borrowers, control, legend rule; 39 (C, 21) Replacements and counterspells; 40 (C, 20) Flips, dice and draw variants; 41 (C, 28) Sacrifice, destroy, look and reveal variants; 42 (C, 37) Cost-reduction components; 43 (C, 21) Specialize; 44 (C, 29) Keyword actions; 45 (C, 29) Singles with a component; ### Defect queue

Every "Seen, not changed" defect of triage runs 1 to 7, the parallel chunks 01 to 16 and the former "Open defects seen and not fixed"
list (which this replaces), deduplicated; defect runs 1 and 2 fixed the rest of triage runs 1 to 5's. One line each: card(s) or code, the defect (rule), where seen (T*n* = triage run *n*,
P*nn* = chunk *nn*, "run *n*" = step 9's run *n*, 8.*n* / 5*x* = older steps), how sure. "Code" = still in the code on 2026-11-23,
"unverified" = not re-checked. "Fold: *batch*" = the fold queue batch of that name fixes it on the way.

**2. Two-player assumptions (`getOpponentId`, first seat).** All right with two players only.

- Notion Thief, Hullbreacher, Alms Collector (`DrawService`), Elesh Norn (`areOpponentPermanentETBTriggersSuppressed`) look at one opponent; Distant Memories asks the first opponent by seat and finds its controller as "the other player"; Infernal Denizen offers the first opponent. P02. Sure. Defect run D12: Notion Thief, Hullbreacher, Alms Collector, Distant Memories fixed; Elesh Norn already fixed (fold run 39); Infernal Denizen left (inside a ForcedCostOrElseEffect fallback: a choose-opponent context or the step-10 merge).
- Shared Fate exiles from `getOpponentId` (the drawer chooses); Abstract Performance, Brilliant Ultimatum, Burning-Rune Demon, Captivating Glance's clash take the first other player. P11. Sure. (Burning-Rune Demon's part fixed in fold run 10b.) Defect run D12: Captivating Glance and every ClashEffect card fixed (the controller chooses); Burning-Rune Demon already fixed; Shared Fate (a prompt inside DrawService), Abstract Performance and Brilliant Ultimatum (choice after the exile) left.
- Death or Glory, Ecological Appreciation take the first other seat; Deliver Unto Evil, Fateseal use `getOpponentId` (the controller chooses, CR 700.2e's reading); Covenant of Minds takes the first non-opponent seat as controller; Triskaidekaphobia declares the first survivor the winner. P12. Sure. (Death or Glory fixed in fold run 14b: the controller chooses the opponent; Ecological Appreciation open.) Fold run 8c: Triskaidekaphobia's "first survivor wins" now lives in `ControllerLosesGameEffectHandler`, which names the winner as `getOpponentId` of the first loser (two-player assumption). Defect run D12: Fateseal and Deliver Unto Evil fixed; Death or Glory and Covenant of Minds already fixed; Ecological Appreciation left (choice after the reveal); Triskaidekaphobia left (multiplayer elimination, CR 800.4a).
- Galactus forces the first opponent on a tie; Norn's Annex's tax and Nahiri's `MustAttackPlayerEffect` count use `getOpponentId`. P07. Sure. Defect run D12: Nahiri fixed (AttackLegalityService.canAttackAPlayer); Galactus left (requiredAttackTargetId as a set at 4 readers); Norn's Annex left (per-defender attack tax).
- ~~`CastingCostService.getAttackPaymentPerCreature` takes the defender as `getOpponentId` (Nils, the other taxes). P06. Sure. Fold: attack-tax.~~ Fixed in fold run 48b.
- Stand or Fall serves only `getOpponentId` ("each defending player") and floats `OnlyChosenCreaturesCanBlockEffect` on every player; Favored Enemy reads the first opponent's library. P08. Sure. (Stand or Fall fixed in fold run 14b; Favored Enemy open.) Defect run D12: Favored Enemy left (needs an as-enters opponent choice).
- ~~Mirri, Weatherlight Duelist limits only the defending player ("each opponent"); Karn, Scion of Urza +1 hands the choice to the first other seat; Wardscale Dragon's defender falls back to it. P09. Sure. Mirri fixed in fold run 5. (Karn fixed in fold run 16.)~~ Defect run D12: Mirri and Karn already fixed (fold runs 5, 16); Wardscale Dragon not a defect for declared attackers (CR 508.5); a creature put onto the battlefield attacking with no target is a new group-2 line.
- Hostile Negotiations (always the first other seat), Memories Returning, Murmurs from Beyond pick the opponent by seat or ask the controller. P13. Sure. (Murmurs from Beyond's part fixed in fold run 10b.) Defect run D12: Memories Returning not a defect (the controller is asked through MemoriesReturningOpponentChoice); Hostile Negotiations left (choice after the piles).
- Zur's Weirding offers the payment only to `getOpponentId` ("any other player"); Verdant Mastery likewise. P15. Sure. Defect run D12: left; Zur's Weirding needs its offer chained over each other player in turn order; Verdant Mastery's choice comes after the search.
- Threats Undetected, Turtles Forever: "an opponent chooses" takes the first opponent. P14. Fairly sure. Defect run D12: left (choice after the reveal; the mid-flow choose-opponent line).
- ~~Seeker of Slaanesh is read as Trove of Temptation: any attack (another opponent, a battle) meets "must attack with at least one creature". P05. Sure. Fold: attack-req.~~ Fixed in fold run 36 (test).

- `MayEffect`'s `DEFENDING_PLAYER` doesn't read the recorded defending player (CR 508.5; Harbor Guardian's kind); Soldevi Heretic derives "target opponent" and targets nobody. 5m. Unverified. Fold: may-draw. Fixed in fold run 5 (the defending player); Soldevi Heretic still open (its ledger row). Defect run D12: Soldevi Heretic left (needs a second, player, target group).
- ~~The two-player "loses the game" declaration doesn't make a draw of simultaneous losses (CR 104.4a). Run 9. Unverified.~~ Already fixed (fold run 8c: a draw when every remaining player loses, CR 104.4a; checked in defect run D12).

Seen in fold run 36b. Sure. Defect run D12: left; needs multi-defender block declaration (CR 509.1): CombatBlockService, the AI and the frontend.
- Ingenious Mastery: "an opponent creates two Treasures and they scry 2" gives the tokens to the first seat and has every opponent scry. Defect run D12. Sure.

- `ReturnCardExiledWithSourceToBattlefieldEffectHandler` puts a creature onto the battlefield attacking with no attack target; its controller should choose one (CR 508.4). Readers then fall back to the first other seat (Wardscale Dragon). Defect run D12. Fairly sure.

**3. Simultaneity and APNAP (CR 101.4, 603.10a, 608.2e).**

- ~~Giant Albatross destroys each creature as its controller answers; needs the unpaid creatures collected across the pay-life prompts and one `DestructionSupport.destroyBatchWithOutcome` at the end. T3, defect runs 1, 2. Code.~~ Fixed in defect run D13.

Code. Also Alpha Brawl's second step (fold run 26). Sure. Defect run D13: left; needs per-recipient handling across sources in DamageSupport.dealSimultaneousDamage, else marking the handlers one event changes nothing observable.
- Disorienting Choice exiles, Eumidian Wastewaker sacrifices or discards at each player's answer. P12. Sure. Defect run D13: Disorienting Choice fixed (APNAP, then exiled together); Eumidian Wastewaker left: needs its discard-or-sacrifice choice made before anything is discarded or sacrificed.

- ~~Hive Mind, Curse of Echoes copy in seat order from the first seat, not APNAP (Curse of Echoes ruling); Hive Mind's optional branch is unreachable. T7. Sure.~~ Fixed in fold run 29 (APNAP; the old record and its branch are gone).

Fixed in fold run 47 for the non-counter cards: every punisher sacrifice is picked in turn and made together (Clackbridge and Innocent Traveler rulings); Brain Gorgers and the other counter cards remain for fold run 47b.~~ Fixed in fold run 47b: Brain Gorgers and the other counter cards sacrifice as each player accepts and counter at once (PLC ruling).

Unverified.~~ Not a defect (defect run D13): inside one handler run with no prompt between, the resolving effect's rules-event batch already makes the exiles one event (Ulamog tests).
- ~~Descent into Madness, Sothera exile the chosen permanents one at a time (Sothera before the next opponent chooses). Runs 8, 9. Unverified.~~ Defect run D13: Sothera fixed (choices collected, one leave batch); Descent into Madness not a defect (test passes on the old code).
- Ol Rin's Searing Light, Gilded Ambusher exile each opponent's choice before the next chooses; Dispersal returns and discards one opponent at a time; The Second Doctor, Kwain draw before the next player chooses. Run 9. Unverified. Defect run D13: Ol Rin's Searing Light fixed; Gilded Ambusher and Dispersal not reached (Sothera pattern); The Second Doctor and Kwain low, left (the draws are one after another in APNAP order anyway, CR 121.2c; only the choices interleave).
- Kairi, Leadership Vacuum, Resounding Wave's cycling return one permanent at a time. Run 10. Unverified. Defect run D13: Resounding Wave not a defect (one handler run, one event; test); Kairi and Leadership Vacuum likely the same, untested.
- Unfinished Business puts its two attachments onto the battlefield one after the other. Run 10. Unverified.
- Mythweaver Poq's simultaneous duplicates are one conjure event each (unsettled). Run 31. Unverified.
- Disorder deals its damage as two events (the players first, then the white creatures) where its one sentence is one event; `addSimultaneousEffects` would join them. Fold run 2. Fairly sure; low impact (a "whenever a source deals damage" trigger sees two events). Defect run D13: left; no reader yet tells two same-source events with different recipients apart, so no failing test.

- An additional time a triggered ability triggers (CR 603.2d) is made by `GameData.TriggerAwareStackList.add` copying the first entry (`copyForNewStackObject`) once it reaches the stack, so the copies keep the first instance's targets and any mode announced before it got there; each instance should choose its own (CR 603.3c, 603.3d). A "choose one at random" now routes each instance on its own (fold run 7). Fold run 7. Unsure (by reading; no doubler-with-targets test checked).
- `ParleyEffect`'s token form (Cutthroat Negotiator, Innocuous Researcher) creates the tokens inside its own handler, so a token-creation prompt pauses it after the draws' order is set and a rerun would reveal and draw again; Storm Fleet Negotiator and Phabine now use the step form. Fold run 7. Unsure (by reading).
- Tribute's "paid" check is `placed >= N`, so a halved tribute (an opponent's Halving Season) reads as unpaid (CR 702.104b: "as specified"). Fold run 22. Code sure, rule unsure. Defect run D13: left for the owner (CR 702.104b and the BNG rulings don't say whether a reduced placement counts as paid).
- Experience counters and `MultiPermanentChoiceHandlerService`'s poison counters name no placer, so Vorinclex and Halving Season don't apply to them. Fold run 22. Sure.
- The 6-argument entering `replaceCounters` (a named placer) skips `appliesToAllPermanents` doublers on opponents' permanents (Primal Vigor's counter half). Fold run 22. Fairly sure.
- `TokenKindReplacements` and `JinnieFayTokenReplacements` read `event.controllerId()`'s battlefield, not the tokens' creator after Crafty Cutpurse; the count replacements resolve with Gather Specimens for creature tokens, though Chatterfang's ruling applies entering-control changes after (CR 616.1). Fold run 22b. Fairly sure.
- The two remaining fixed-order `getTokenMultiplier` callers (`CreateTokensAttachedToControlledCreaturesEffectHandler`, `ReturnTargetCreatureToHandAndCreateTokenCopyEffectHandler`) skip the ordered token event. Fold run 22b. Code.
- The recipient damage multipliers (Gisela, Castigator, floated) are one pool option and the controller and source multipliers another, so a "plus N" can't be ordered between two of them (CR 616.1). Fold run 25. Sure of the code.
- ~~Blind Fury's `getCombatDamageToCreatureMultiplier` skips Maro's Gone Nuts. Fold run 25. Sure.~~ Fixed in fold run 40b.
- Precursor Golem, Ink-Treader Nephilim: the copies' controller doesn't choose the order they go on the stack (rulings). Seen in fold run 29. Sure.
- `PutExiledCardsIntoOwnersGraveyardEffect` (Covenant of Minds, Dakra Mystic) gives no CR 404.3 order prompt. Run 45. Sure, low.
- ~~Betrayal of Flesh, Solar Tide, Phyrexian Tribute: the escalate/entwine sacrifice payer and `payMultipleSacrificeCost` sacrifice their cost permanents one at a time, not as one event (CR 603.10a). Run 49b. Fairly sure.~~ Fixed in defect run D13.
- Eumidian Wastewaker counts a sacrificed land token, or a permanent that is a land only on the battlefield (Ashaya), as a land card put into a graveyard (ruling). Defect run D13. Sure.
- ~~Cut the Tethers (`ReturnMatchingPermanentsUnlessOwnerPaysEffectHandler`) bounces at each owner's answer, not together after the last (CR 101.4). Defect run D13. Sure.~~ Fixed in step 10 run U6 (`UnlessPaysEffect.forEach`).
- Ezuri's Predation: "those creatures" are read at the fight, not when counted (CR 608.2h). Defect run D13. Low.
- Flame Spill, Pigment Storm, Ravenous Tyrannosaurus, Megatron, Ram Through: the excess ignores damage from other sources dealt at the same time (a simultaneous step's damage to the same creature, CR 120.4a); only `SequenceEffect.simultaneous` could combine them, and none of these cards does. Step 10 run X. Sure of the code, low.
- `AttackLegalityService.isCantAttackDueToGlobalRestriction`'s planechase branch judges a plane's `CreaturesCantAttackUnlessPredicateEffect` exemption with no filter context (the context it builds is used only by the attack-or-block branch), so a source-relative exemption (Ensnaring Bridge's hand size, `youControl`) on a plane would misjudge; no plane uses one today. Step 10 run S. Sure of the code, latent.

- `DealDamageTo{AnyTarget,Players,TargetCreature}Effect` lose a player hit from the step result when a replacement-order prompt reruns them. Step 7. Unverified. Defect run D14: left; 3+ players only (Chandra, Roaring Flame −7); fix: keep the finished StepOutcome in DamageJournal.Slot.Done.

Fold run 2. Fairly sure (the modified event, drawing two, occurs instead of the draw and is still this instruction's, CR 614.6). Defect run D14: 'that card' is right as coded (Pact Weapon ruling); 'if you do' / 'that many' after a draw replaced by draws (Baral, Trade Route Envoy, The Royal Scions) waits for the owner; Struggle for Project Purity's "for each card drawn this way" is the same question (its record, `EachOpponentDrawsCardEffect`, counts a replacement's extra cards; step 10 run D left it there).

**5. Wrong targets and choices.**

- `ChoosePermanentsEffect` keeps a lone chosen permanent as the source's chosen permanent, so Deepfathom Echo (if folded onto it) or any copier choosing its model would leave a "the chosen creature" card's copy with a choice it never made (Dauntless Bodyguard). Fold run 4. Sure of the code. Defect run D15: left; Zenos yae Galvus relies on the source's chosen-permanent write, which leaks into a later copy (CR 607.2d, 707.7): needs choices keyed by their linking ability.
- `CardSubject.TriggeringCards` names a card only in the stay its event put it in, so `bindCardSubjects` reads no type for a discarded card that was exiled instead or has left the graveyard, where the text reads last-known characteristics (CR 608.2h; Diviner of Fates' fold). Fold run 4. Sure of the code. Defect run D15: already fixed (fold run 17d); use triggeringCardsAsTheyLastExisted when Diviner of Fates is folded.

- `MayCastHandlerService.castRevealedCardWithoutPaying`, `SplitCardCastSupport`'s `payManaCost=false` casts and `LibraryChoiceHandlerService`'s search casts pay no cost increase (CR 601.2f); move them onto `payFreeCastCostIncreases` and its mana window. Seen in fold run 29d. Sure. Defect run D17: left for a free-cast flow run.

- The free-cast queue asks for the cost-increase mana before modes and targets; a spell then left with no legal target leaves the mana in the pool. Seen in fold run 29d. Sure of the code. Defect run D17: left for a free-cast flow run.
- ~~`CastingCostService.computeTargetBasedCostReduction` returns the first non-zero category, so a per-target reduction hides a spell's own "if it targets" reduction (CR 601.2f). Run 42. Sure of the code.~~ Fixed in defect run D17.
- ~~Infectious Curse: the playability preview of its cost reduction runs only for instants and sorceries, so a Curse cast at the cursed player isn't shown as playable at the reduced cost. Run 42. Sure of the code.~~ Fixed in defect run D17.
- The per-target cost readers have no lost-abilities or face-down check (CR 613.1f, 708.2a). Run 42. Sure. Defect run D17: partly fixed by defect run D11 (active statics); Strong Back and Infectious Curse readers still read printed statics, no testable card.
- `XValueChoiceAiStrategy` answers a life payment with its maximum, so the AI pays its whole life (Plunge into Darkness, Vizkopa Confessor; CR 119.4). Run 46. Sure of the code. Defect run D17: an AI choice, not a rules error (CR 119.4); needs a life-payment marker on XValueChoice.
- ~~Depala, Pilot Exemplar: its pay-{X} pool counts artifact-only and Myr-only mana, which can't pay this ability. Run 46. Sure of the code.~~ Already fixed (fold run 46b: Depala pays through calculateMaxXForResolutionCost; checked in defect run D17).
- ~~Rose Room Treasurer: "you may pay {X}. When you do" has no `MayEffect`, so declining (X = 0) still makes the targeted reflexive ability (CR 603.12). Run 46b. Fairly sure, low.~~ Fixed in defect run D17.
- `AbilityActivationService.getActivatedAbilityAdditionalGenericCost`: with a {0} equip or power-up cost, the tax is returned without cost reductions applied (CR 601.2f). Run 49. Fairly sure of the code. Defect run D17: left, low; needs a three-card test.
- ~~Hand-ability availability may not show a free cycle (Gavi) as playable without mana. Run 49. Unverified.~~ Not a defect (defect run D17): no availability check gates hand abilities, and Gavi's free cycle works (test).
- ~~King Narfi's Betrayal: "as though it were mana of any color" is implemented as any-type spending, so coloured mana pays {C} (CR 609.4b). Run 30b. Fairly sure.~~ Not reproduced in fold run 30c: Narfi's exile cast already pays as any colour for a {C} cost. Closed.
- ~~Errant Minion, Power Leak pay from the pool with `ManaCost("{0}").pay`, not `payResolutionCost`, so restricted mana may be judged differently (CR 118.12). Run 46c. Unsure.~~ Fixed in defect run D17.
- ~~Temporal Extortion: a payer whose half life is 0 isn't offered the payment, though CR 119.4 allows paying 0 life. Run 47b. Sure of the code, low.~~ Fixed in defect run D17.
- "Mana of any type" exile and graveyard permissions (`withAnyManaType`, `spendingManaOfAnyType`: Mnemonic Betrayal, Gonti, Dire Fleet Daredevil and others) are paid as any colour when the cost has {C} (`SpellCastingService` `anyColorForColoredSymbols`), so coloured mana can't pay {C} (CR 106.1b, 609.4b). Needs an any-type / any-colour mode on those permissions (about 30 cards); check the Whispersteel / Xanathar line against it. Run 30c (Mnemonic Betrayal probe). Sure. Defect run D17: left; about 30 permissions need an any-type versus any-colour mode.
- ~~Kaya's Guile and the other `EntwineManaCost` cards are refused in exile free casts, though additional costs apply to a free cast (CR 118.9d). Run 49b. Sure of the code.~~ Fixed in fold run 50 (`EscalateCost.entwine` + `requiredOrAllModes` picker).
- Flotsam // Jetsam (Jetsam) refuses Bone Shards, Minion Missile and Souls of the Lost instead of letting their sacrifice-or-discard cost be paid (CR 118.9d); before fold run 49b it cast them with the cost skipped. Run 49b. Sure of the code. Defect run D17: left; the graveyard cast flow needs a sacrifice-or-discard payment step.
- ~~Every `PayLifeCost` card: battlefield and hand activation validation checks `canPlayerLifeChange` but not `canPlayerLoseLife` (`PlayersCantLoseLifeThisTurnEffect`), while the graveyard path checks both (CR 119.8). Run 47c. Fairly sure from the code, not tested.~~ Fixed in defect run D17.
- Gargantuan Gorilla, Bronze Bombshell, Elder Spawn: damage from a source sacrificed earlier in the same resolution loses Aura-granted keywords (`sourceHasKeyword` reads the trigger-time snapshot; CR 608.2h). Run 48. Sure of the code. Defect run D17: left; needs Aura-granted keywords from the snapshot taken when the source left; not verified.
- ~~`MayPayManaEffect` asks a payer who can't pay (406 cards; CR 118.3, 608.2d). Run 48. Sure. Defect run D17: left for the owner: skipping the prompt for a payer who can't pay even with mana abilities would rewrite about 500 card tests that add mana while it is open; today accepting without the mana counts as declining, so the game ends the same. Owner's decision 2026-11-25: no question only when the payer has no way at all to pay (cards in every zone included); when unsure, ask. For step 10 run U3.~~ Fixed in step 10 run U3a: `ResolutionCostPaymentService.couldPossiblyPay` asks unless no payment is possible at all (owner's rule), every single-payer "may pay" is an `UnlessPaysEffect`, and a hand mana ability (Spirit Guides) may be activated while asked; the any-player mana forms (Rhystic Cave, Scarwood Bandits, …) follow since step 10 run U3b' (each payer judged alone, `RhysticTutorTest.playerWithNoWayToPayIsNotAsked`).
- ~~`UnlessPaysEffectHandler.cannotBePaid` (as `CounterUnlessPaysEffectHandler` before it) skips the question when the pool and `PotentialManaService.buildVirtualManaPool` (battlefield mana) can't pay; mana from other zones (Elvish / Simian Spirit Guide in hand) isn't counted, so under the owner's rule of 2026-11-25 (no question only when no payment is possible at all) it must ask there, if the engine lets such mana pay a resolution-time cost. Seen by the orchestrator at step 10 run U1; for run U3's shared "can pay" check. Sure of the code, unverified in play.~~ Fixed in step 10 run U3a: `ResolutionCostPaymentService.couldPossiblyPay` asks unless no payment is possible at all (owner's rule), every single-payer "may pay" is an `UnlessPaysEffect`, and a hand mana ability (Spirit Guides) may be activated while asked.
- Collective Effort and other tap-escalate spells cast from a graveyard through a flashback grant pay no escalate taps (CR 702.120a). Run 50. Fairly sure from the code. Defect run D17: confirmed from the code; add validateEscalateTapCost / payEscalateTapCost beside the tapMultipleCost pair, with a flashback test.
- ~~`OutsideGameNormalCostCastSupport`'s modal path (maximum `choicesMax + 1`) doesn't use `requiredOrAllModes`, so an entwine card's in-between mode count is refused only by the hand path's check. Run 50. Sure of the code.~~ Not a defect (defect run D17): every printed entwine spell has two modes, so choicesMax + 1 is always every mode.
- ~~Resolution-time "pay N life" options in `MayAbilityHandlerService`, `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService` and about 15 handlers check `canPlayerLifeChange` but not `canPlayerLoseLife`, so a per-player "can't lose life" doesn't stop the payment (CR 119.8). Defect run D17. Sure of the code. The counter-unless-pays life ransoms (ward—pay N life, "{2}, Pay 2 life") moved onto `UnlessPaysEffect`, which checks `canPlayerLoseLife` at resolution and at the answer (step 10 run U1); the rest stay.~~ Fixed in defect run D25: every resolution-time life check is `ResolutionCostPaymentService.canPayLife` (or `canPlayerLoseLife` for a running total): Sylvan Library, Lim-Dûl's Vault, Zur's Weirding, shocklands, Killing Wave, the pay-life-per-card looks and searches included.
- Chisei, Heart of Oceans' `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` counter removal may take the first kind of counter instead of letting the payer choose. Defect run D17. Unverified.
- Development (Research // Development): two or more opponents letting its controller draw in one round draw one card, not one per
  accepting opponent (CR 118.12a: each paid cost is that player's; its 2006 rulings don't settle it). Kept one per round (no change in
  play), pinned (`ResearchDevelopmentTest.twoOpponentsChoosingTheDrawInOneRoundDrawOneCard`); owner to review. Step 10 run U3c. Sure of the
  code.

  creature", Blood Funnel, Mana Vortex and now Brain Gorgers) asks the pick even when only one permanent could be sacrificed (the "one
  possible answer is made unasked" convention, CR 608.2d); the paid-together path picks it unasked. Step 10 run U3c. Sure; cosmetic.

  text of `TapMultiplePermanentsCost` names no permanent kind (its filter has no label). Step 10 run U3c. Sure; cosmetic.

- ~~Dalkovan Outrider, Thorna and Twigtooth: the perpetual +X/+X is a static ability on an exchanged card, removed by "loses all abilities"; it is a layer-7c change (CR 613.4c, run 28's form). P16. - ~~`BecomeEnchantmentEffect` resets the card to `getOriginalCard()`, which `CloneService` leaves as the copier's: a Clone of Opal Acrolith becomes a 0/0 Clone and later copy effects drop. T6. Sure / fairly.~~ Fixed in defect run D18.

T6. Sure of the code. Fold: copy-params. Fixed in fold run 4: Unstable Shapeshifter is `BecomeCopyOfSubjectEffect`, whose permanent path reads `PermanentCopierService.copiableCard`.~~ Already fixed (fold run 4; checked in defect run D18).

Defect run D18: left; no ruling settles it.

- Thought Partition and Hydroponics Architect write colour, mana cost, supertype and subtype into runtime copies (layer 1, CR 613). Fold run 12b. Sure of the code. Defect run D18: left; needs perpetual colour, set-mana-cost and supertype stores with their readers.

- ~~Energybending: its own-type skip reads printed types, so under Blood Moon a Taiga misses Forest (CR 305.7, 613.7). Seen in fold run 32. Fairly sure.~~ Fixed in defect run D18.
- ~~`GrantBasicLandTypeToTargetEffect`'s in-addition path grants an explicit mana ability beside the intrinsic one, so a land that already had the type has it twice (CR 305.6). Seen in fold run 32. Sure of the code, low.~~ Not a defect (defect run D18): missingIntrinsicManaAbilities counts the granted mana ability as present, so no second one appears (CR 305.6).
- Starfield of Nyx: `isAnimatedByStarfield` makes a battlefield enchantment a creature ignoring later layer-4 type removals, and `wouldEnterAsCreature` ignores Starfield (Torpor Orb). Seen in fold run 32b. Sure of the code, low. Defect run D18: the later layer-4 type removal half fixed (Starfield override removed from isCreatureWithBonus); wouldEnterAsCreature ignoring Starfield left.
- ~~Titania's Song: its leave-the-battlefield continuation is keyed by `losesAllAbilities`, which only it sets; a second user would need its own marker. Seen in fold run 32b. Sure, low.~~ Not a defect today (defect run D18): only Titania's Song sets the marker.
- `snapshotEnterTappedTypes` / `applyEnterTappedEffects` match printed card types, not the permanent as it would exist on the battlefield (CR 614.12; e.g. an artifact animated by March of the Machines vs Root Maze's type set). Seen in fold run 35b. Sure of the code, untested. Defect run D18: the type-only paths fixed (entersWithCardType, CR 614.12); the filter path is a new line.
- Ability borrowers in a CR 613.8b dependency loop are approximated by assembly order, so a three-borrower cycle can miss a lender's borrowed abilities. Seen in fold run 38. Sure of the code, rare. Defect run D18: left; needs the borrower assembly ordered by dependency (CR 613.8b).
- ~~Guildscorn Ward: `ProtectionFromMulticoloredEffect(attachedCreature())` also protects the Aura itself (the colour-count readers ignore `protectionSubject()`). Run 37. Fairly sure.~~ Fixed in defect run D18.
- `ProtectionFromColorsEffectHandler` gives a self-subject fixed protection to the host when its source is attached (a bestowed or reconfigured creature with printed protection); CR 702.16a makes it the object's own. Run 37b. Sure of the code; no card reaches it. Defect run D18: left; no card reaches it.
- Mask of Law and Grace, Shield of Duty and Reason, Strength of Isolation and Strength of Lunacy stay exempt from their own protection when recoloured, though they have no "doesn't remove" text (CR 702.16c). Run 37b. Fairly sure. Defect run D18: left; needs a 'doesn't remove this Aura' component on ProtectionFromColorsEffect, set by the Ward cards.
- ~~Veil of Summer, Mondo Gecko, Skrelv, Sungold Sentinel: their hexproof-from-colour floats survive a later "loses all abilities" (CR 613.1f). Run 6c. Sure of the code.~~ Fixed in defect run D18.
- ~~`ControlledCreaturesEnterWithAdditionalCountersEffect`'s `allCreatures` path, like Dearly Departed's reader, checks creature-ness from the printed card, not the permanent as it would exist (CR 614.12). Run 24b. Sure of the code. Fold: enter-counters.~~ Fixed in fold run 24c.
- ~~Sage of Fables, Bramblewood Paragon, Metallic Mimic, Oona's Blackguard: the subtype path of `ControlledCreaturesEnterWithAdditionalCountersEffect` doesn't check that the entering permanent is a creature, so a noncreature Kindred permanent of the type gets counters (Oracle "… creature you control"). Run 24c. Likely, untested.~~ Fixed in defect run D18.
- Kalain and Coin of Mastery read cast-time mana snapshots keyed by card id, so a copy of a permanent spell or a countered spell that later enters may read a stale amount (CR 707.10). Run 24c. Unverified.
- `BecomeCreatureEffect` (Opal Acrolith, Hidden Stag, 24 cards) replaces the card instead of floating a layer-4 and layer-7b effect, so a later copy effect drops the type change (CR 613.1a, 613.1d). Defect run D18. Sure of the code.
- `PerpetuallyGrantStaticEffectToTargetCreatureEffectHandler`, `PerpetuallyGrantStaticEffectToSourceEffectHandler`, `DealDamageToAnyTargetThenPerpetuallyGrantStaticEffectsIfCreatureDamagedEffectHandler`, `PerpetuallyBecomeCreatureEffectHandler` and `ExileArtifactThenSeekArtifactAndPerpetuallyBecomeCreatureEffectHandler` write perpetual changes into an exchanged card, which a copy then copies (CR 707.2). Defect run D18. Sure of the code.
- `EnterPermanentsOfTypesTappedEffect.matching` filters judge the entering permanent as it is, not as it would exist on the battlefield (CR 614.12). Defect run D18. Unverified.
- `TextChangeTransformer` rewrites change effect identity, so a text-changed targeted trigger can lose its target group. Defect run D18. Unsure.
- Whether a text change made to a spell on the stack (Sleight of Mind on an instant, `spellTextReplacements`) reaches its resolution was not traced. Defect run D18. Unverified.

**8. Logs and hidden information.**

- ~~Hydroponics Architect's log names the random library card to every player (CR 400.2). P16. Sure.~~ Fixed in D10: "A random land card in X's library perpetually becomes ...".
- ~~Expert-Level Safe's mismatch uses `ExileTopCardsToSourceEffect(1, true)` (may look) ...~~ Not a defect in behaviour (D10): the look is granted only to a spell's exile or by the card's printed effects, so the engine-built mismatch exile gave none; it now says `faceDownWithoutLookPermission(1)`, as the ruling does, with a guard test.
- ~~`DraftCardIntoHandEffect` logs the drafted name to every player. P08. Sure.~~ Fixed in D10 with every spellbook path into a hand: the draft mode label (`ChooseModeChoice.secret`), `SpellbookDraftChoice`, `SpellbookCardChoice`, the draft-twice and artifact-creature draft lines say "a card".
- ~~Second person in the shared log: Chitinous Crawler and the Skeleton duplicate (run 31), Storyweave (P10), Vodalian Tide Mage, Vesuvan Mist, Warzone Duplicator, Bind to Secrecy, Futurist Spellthief, Goblin Morale Sergeant, Sheoldred's Assimilator, Pampered Loamfrill, Fallaji (P16). Code.~~ Fixed in D10: the twelve handlers name the player.
- ~~Malik's two choices and Vault 11's secret votes are never revealed or logged. P07, P10. Low. Fold: object-vote (Vault 11; fold run 11 left it); Malik isn't a vote, its half goes with the conditional CHOOSE-slot row. Vault 11's half fixed in fold run 11b (revealed in one line after the last vote, once); Malik's half open.~~ Malik fixed in defect run D12 (choices revealed and logged); Vault 11 already fixed (fold run 11b).
- ~~Molten Sentry and Rakdos log "wins / loses the coin flip" for flips nobody wins (CR 705.2). P04. Log only.~~ Fixed in D10: "X's coin flip for ... comes up heads / tails".
- ~~The Mycosynth Gardens and Valki log nothing when they become a copy. T6. Low. Mycosynth Gardens logs the survivor's line since fold run 4; Valki still logs nothing.~~ Valki fixed in fold run 50b.
- ~~`MayCopyHandlerService.handleBecomeCopyChoice` logs "'s copy target is no longer on the battlefield." when the copy succeeds. 5m. Code.~~ Fixed in D10: "X becomes a copy of Y."
- ~~Several kept Grant handlers name a floating effect's source by `entry.getCard().getName()` (CR 708.2). Run 18. Unverified.~~ Not a defect (D10): `GameData.addFloatingEffect` relabels every label naming the resolving entry's card while its source is face down (`withPublicSourceName`).
- ~~Moonring Mirror's and Duplicity's hand exile keep a source-linked face-down card's implicit look (CR 406.3); the view's look and `mayLookAtExiledCard` answer one question twice. Runs 6, 13. Unverified.~~ Fixed in defect run D12.
- ~~An entry from face-down exile that asks an Aura host names the card (CR 406.3). Run 10. Unverified.~~ Not a defect (defect run D12): the Aura enters face up into a public zone and its controller chooses the host as it enters (CR 400.2, 303.4f).
- `cardsThisWay(REVEAL)` names a card a later step reordered within its library (CR 701.20d); no card reads it so. Run 13. Unverified. Defect run D12: left; latent, no card.
- ~~Laquatus's Creativity with an empty hand runs a draw and a discard of 0; what they log wasn't checked. Run 33. Unverified.~~ Not a defect (D10): the draw logs nothing, the discard "X discards 0 cards.", which tells nothing a hand count doesn't (CR 402.3).
- ~~Found in D10: `MayCopyHandlerService.handleBecomeCopyChoice` copies `targetPerm.getCard()`, not `PermanentCopierService.cardToCopy` (a face-down target, CR 707.2, 708.2; Cryptoplasm, Vesuvan Doppelganger, Protean Thaumaturge, Artisan of Forms). Code.~~ Fixed in defect run D12.
- Found in D10: Support Skyforge's spellbook selection exiles the two cards not chosen face up and logs their names before the follow-up removes them (`LibraryChoiceHandlerService` rest-to-exile branch); a spellbook card not drafted goes nowhere. Code, low. Defect run D12: left; the spellbook selection needs a destination where the unchosen cards leave the game.
- ~~Found in D10: Juggle the Performance conjures seven duplicates from a library of fewer than seven cards (picks with replacement); "each of seven random cards" may mean at most the library's size. No ruling. Unsure.~~ Fixed in defect run D12.
- ~~Found in D10: a hidden choice of "up to" N that chooses none, and a random pick from a hand or library that finds none, log nothing, where a pick logs a line (Racketeer Boss, Hydroponics Architect). Low.~~ Not a defect (defect run D12): no line is owed when nothing is chosen or found, and nothing hidden is shown.
- ~~Absorb Energy reads a face-down target spell's printed types (CR 708.2). Fold run 12b. Unverified.~~ Fixed in defect run D12.
- ~~`SeekLibraryEffect` to a hand logs no line, while `SeekCardsToHandEffect` logs "seeks N cards". Fold run 20. Low.~~ Fixed in defect run D12.
- `ChooseCardEffectHandler` logs a chosen card unnamed whenever it is in a hand (fold run 12e's zone check), even a revealed one; keep hidden-ness on the subject (fold run 20's note: a zone-based prompt check broke Addle and Intuition). Fold run 12e merge. Sure, low (log only). Defect run D12: left; over-redaction only, needs revealed-ness on the card subject.
- A free cast refused by Teferi or Rule of Law logs "has no legal targets". Fold run 18c. Low. Defect run D12: left; about 15 free-cast sites need the refusal reason from canCastDuringResolution.
- `SetBasePowerToughnessEffectHandler`'s set and player-target log lines print "null" for a null side. No current card. Seen in fold run 31b. Sure. Defect run D12: left; no card reaches either branch.
- Shadow of Doubt logs nothing since fold run 34: `GrantStaticEffectToPlayerEffect` has no log line. Seen in fold run 34. Sure, low. Defect run D12: left; a line in GrantStaticEffectToPlayerEffect changes every user's log.
- `getRevealedOpponentHand` returns only the first opponent's hand (multiplayer). Seen in fold run 35. Sure of the code. Defect run D12: left; needs a hand per opponent in GameStateMessage and the frontend.

- `PerpetuallyBoostSourceEffect`: the graveyard path rewrites the card's printed P/T instead of using the perpetual store, so a later perpetual base-P/T change overrides the boost. Defect run D23, fairly sure.
- Rothga (`PerpetuallyBoostTriggeringCardByPowerEffect`): a face-down creature spell's X is read from the real card, not its face-down characteristics (CR 708.2). Defect run D23, unsure.

- Missing feature (owner): no client chooser for an "[A] or [B]" additional cast cost (`AnyOfCost` as `addCastingCost`). A human
  gets the default alternative (mana, else life) and the hand-cast client sends no discard choice for **any** additional discard
  cost, so Bone Shards, Demand Answers, Minion Missile and Souls of the Lost can't be cast from the UI at all (CR 601.2b: the caster
  announces the alternative). Needs a generic `CardView` list of alternatives and a picker routing to the discard, permanent and
  graveyard pickers; `PlayCardRequest` already carries every field. Orchestrator's decision Q3 for step 10 run U7; sure.
- The free-cast additional-cost chooser ("Choose how to pay the additional cost.") is asked after the targets, not as the spell is
  announced (CR 601.2b, before 601.2c). It changes play for none of these cards. Orchestrator's decision Q6 for step 10 run U7; sure.
- A spell played from a graveyard without paying its mana cost (`MayCastHandlerService.handlePlayFromGraveyardChoice`: Horde of
  Notions and the other users) pays no additional cast cost: it refuses a discard-or-sacrifice one (Bone Shards' shape) and casts
  Bitter Triumph, Final Payment and Dusk Mangler with theirs skipped (CR 601.2f, 601.2h). Kept as it was in step 10 run U7a
  (seen, not changed). Sure of the code.
- A library-top cast of Bitter Triumph with cards in hand is refused: the library-top request carries no discard choice, so only the
  empty-hand life payment is accepted (CR 601.2b). Step 10 run U7a (seen, not changed; U7b kept the library-top path's one request
  field, the sacrifice ids, and added no discard field). Sure of the code.
- An exile cast of a "discard a card or pay {m}" spell (Lightning Axe, Pumpkin Bombardment, Titania) can't choose the discard: no discard
  index reaches the exile path (`playCardFromExile` takes only permanent ids), so it always pays the mana (CR 601.2b). Needs the
  request's `discardHandCardIndex` threaded through the exile overloads and `ExileCastReplay`. Step 10 run U7b (seen, not changed). Sure.
- A free cast (`ExileFreeCastQueueSupport`) doesn't offer an "[A] or [B]" cost's graveyard-exile or forage alternative: there is no
  free-cast graveyard picker, so a free Soaring Stoneglider or Feed the Cycle pays its mana alternative or is skipped (CR 601.2b). Needs a
  graveyard choice context for the prepared spell (and a Food picker for forage). Step 10 run U7b / U7c (seen, not changed). Sure.
- Exile and library-top casts still refuse tap-, blight- and graveyard-exile-or-mana spells (Disruption Protocol, Wild Unraveling,
  Soaring Stoneglider) as "not supported from this zone"; library top takes only sacrifice-or-mana. As before step 10 run U7b. Sure.
- `BoostEffect.targetPermanent` (Vehicle boosts: Fang Guardian, Captain America's Motorcycle, Blacksmith's Skill, Tributary Vaulter)
  declares a harmful target spec whatever the sign of the boost, so the AI's target prior treats a pump of its own creature or Vehicle
  as an attack; derive the prior from the sign as `BoostEffect.target` does. AI only. Orchestrator's decision Q6 for step 10 run Boost
  B1 (parity kept); sure.
- Battle cry is built on "creatures you control" (B2 keeps it), but CR 702.91a gives +1/+0 to "each other attacking creature", a
  teammate's attackers too (Two-Headed Giant). Orchestrator's decision Q7 for step 10 run Boost B1; sure of the rule.
- Text-changing effects (CR 612.2) reach a source-reading boost's amounts and target filter only: not a self or named-permanent
  boost's amounts ("+1/+1 for each black creature"), not `targetPermanent`'s filter (Buccaneer's Bravado's "target Pirate"), not the
  mass boosts' set filters. Orchestrator's decision Q8 for step 10 run Boost B1 (parity); sure of the code.
- `TriggeredAbilityQueueService.triggerOrderLabel` names a source-reading boost with no target "target creature gets +N/+M" (Sigardian
  Zealot, Mirkwood Trapper's second trigger, Zenos yae Galvus); a label only. Step 10 run Boost B1 (seen, not changed); sure.
- `BoostEffectHandler`'s refresh of the boosted permanent's waiting abilities' snapshots changes nothing observable: a departing
  permanent refreshes every waiting entry's snapshot itself (`PermanentRemovalService`), and a live source is read live. Cleanup
  candidate, not a defect (step 10 run Boost B1's Q3 test passed on the old code). Sure.
- "Attacking creatures get" / "other attacking creatures get" written as `BoostEffect.creaturesYouControl(…, attacking)` (Iroh,
  Firebending Instructor, Kangee's Lieutenant, Motivated Pony, Targ Nar, Honored Crop-Captain and others) leaves out a teammate's
  attackers (Two-Headed Giant), as battle cry does (Q7); `allCreatures(…, attacking)` would say it. Same in two-player games. Step 10
  run Boost B2 (seen, not changed: parity); sure of the wording.
- ~~`DamageSupport.resolveAnyTargetDamage`'s player branch applies the next-instant-or-sorcery redirect shield (Aegis of Honor,
  `applyNextInstantOrSorceryDamageRedirectShield`) before the player's damage pool, which also offers it as a pool option: one shield,
  so it isn't applied twice, but the affected player isn't asked the order beside another prevention or redirect (CR 616.1). Step 10
  run Damage P (seen, not changed; orchestrator's decision Q6). Sure of the code.~~ Fixed in defect run D26 (the pool applies it once; `SetAndPlayerDamagePoolTest.Aegis`).
- ~~The set damage handlers (`DealDamageToEachTargetEffect`, `DealDamageToPermanentsTargetControlsEffect`, the mass and
  defending-player records) log the source by `getCard().getName()`, bypassing `FaceDownVisibility` (CR 708.2a), and the
  each-matching, each-target and defending-player handlers begin no DEAL_DAMAGE step: the defect run before M1. Step 10 run Damage P
  (plan's survey, seen, not changed). Sure of the code.~~ Fixed in defect run D26 (the lines went with the protection pre-checks; mass begins its step too; `SetAndPlayerDamagePoolTest.FaceDownSource`, `StepResultsTest.DealDamage`).
- The hard AI gives set damage no value: `DealDamageEffect.toEach` / `toEachTarget` answer `canDamageCreatures()` and
  `canDamagePlayers()` false (as the absorbed records were no `DamageDealingEffect`), so Simoon scores the same with or without an
  opposing creature (`SpellEvaluatorTest.setDamageSweeperIsNotScoredAsTargetedDamage` pins it) and no sweeper branch reads them; M2's
  `BoardWipeEffect` view could cover `toEach`. Step 10 run Damage M1 (seen, not changed: AI parity). Sure. Step 10 run Damage M2: a set over
  every player's permanents (`controlledBy(eachPlayer(), …)`, M1's all-player each-matching cards included) is now the AI's board
  sweep (`DealDamageEffect.sweepsBoard()`, the hard AI's board-wipe value, `BoardEvaluator`, the easy AI); a set over one player's
  permanents or each target (Simoon) is still unscored.
- `CloneService.exileCopiedCardAbility` (Superior Spider-Man, Activated Sleeper, The Mimeoplasm and the other "enter as a copy of a
  creature card in a graveyard; when you do, exile that card" cards) names the copied card by `setTriggeringCardId` with no graveyard
  stay, so a card that left the graveyard and came back before the reflexive ability resolves is still exiled; it is a new object
  (CR 400.7). Record the stay as the ability is made (`setTriggeringCard(id, graveyardEntryVersion)`), failing-first. Step 10 run A
  (seen, not changed: the absorbed record behaved the same). Sure of the code, not tested.
- `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService`'s inline mana-ability branch for `DrawCardEffect` / `ManaAbilityCardDrawingEffect` draws for the
  activating player only, ignoring `player()`; latent (an ability with a card draw is never a mana ability here,
  `AbilityActivationService.movesCardToOrFromLibrary`, CR 605.1a), so likely dead code to delete. Step 10 run D (seen, not changed). Sure
  of the code.
- `EachOtherPlayerDrawsCardEffectHandler` (the 11 gift cards) draws in seating order from the first seat, not in APNAP order (CR 101.4);
  fold with the gift opponent. Step 10 run D (seen, not changed). Sure of the code.
- The hard AI prices "each opponent draws" (`SpellEvaluator.drawScore`) as one opponent's cards, not one set per opponent in a
  multiplayer game. AI only. Step 10 run D (seen, not changed). Sure.
- `TargetPolarityClassifier` has no entry for `ChooseOneForTargetCreatureEffect` and reads `ChooseOneForTargetPermanentEffect` as
  NEUTRAL, though some modes harm the target (Urborg, Walking Sponge, one of Shaper Parasite's): the AI's targeting for these 15
  cards ignores the modes. AI only; it blocks folding the pair (and the subtype grant's polarity). Step 10 run PO (seen, not
  changed). Sure of the code, not of the AI's resulting choices.
- `TheGrimCaptainsLockerTest.doesNotGrantEscapeToNonCreatureCards` asserts a Grizzly Bears in a graveyard that holds only a Shock
  and never tries to cast the Shock with escape, so it doesn't test what its name says. Test only. Step 10 run PO (seen, not
  changed). Sure of the assertion; not checked why it passes.
- Rikku, Resourceful Guardian: "until end of turn, that creature can't be blocked by creatures your opponents control" floats
  a granted `CantBeBlockedByCreaturesMatchingPredicateEffect` ability (`MakeCreatureUnblockableEffect.byCreaturesYourOpponentsControl()`),
  so a later "loses all abilities" ends it, though the effect gives no ability (CR 611.2a, 613.11). Needs a block-legality read of
  a floating blocker restriction on the attacker (`BlockLegalityService.buildAttackerFacts`, `CombatHelper`); run U moved only the
  plain "can't be blocked" durations onto the floating rule. Step 10 run U (seen, not changed). Sure of the code and the rule.
- Sarulf, Realm Eater: its exile (`ExileOtherNonlandPermanentsWithManaValueAtMostEffectHandler`) moves each permanent with its own
  `removePermanentToExile`, not as one leave batch, so "whenever one or more ... leave" abilities see several events (one
  instruction moves them as one event: Conventions, "Rules a fold must keep"). Step 10 run C1 (seen, not changed). Sure of the code; no test.
- `ExileTargetPermanentEffectHandler`: a plain `target()` over several targets ("exile up to two target creatures") exiles them
  one by one with no leave batch, and a non-set several-permanent subject (`allOf(source(), target())`, `allTargets()`) gets the
  leave batch but not the one rules-event occurrence a set has since run K2 (CR 603.2c, 603.10a: one instruction, one event).
  Step 10 run K (seen, not changed). Sure of the code; no card test shows it.
- The AI scores "destroy all [X] target player controls" (`DestroyTargetPermanentEffect.allTargetPlayerControls`) over both
  battlefields, its own included (`SpellEvaluator.evaluateDestroyAllValue`, parity with the old wipe record). AI only, no rule.
  Step 10 run K (seen, not changed). Sure.

named them (rows, batches):
